STUDIO LAB - GENRE KNOWLEDGE  (version 2.3.1, 355 genres)

How to read it:
  Patterns are one bar of 16 steps (16th notes). Drums: X = accent, x = hit, . = rest.
  Bass: R = root, O = octave, F = fifth, - = hold. Chords / lead: x = a note starts, - = held.
  Chord progressions are scale degrees as roman numerals (minor: i ii° III iv v VI VII).
  Song structure: each section, its length and which parts play; [flags] = what the section does
  (drop, build, filter_in, layer_in/out, alt = variation, fade, gap = a drop-out ...).
  Sounds: the main sound of each part, then the sounds it can switch to from song to song.

house   [house]
------------------------------------------------------------------------
Classic / NYC house: four-on-the-floor 909, the bouncy M1 organ bass, offbeat piano and organ stabs, warm pads, pumping sidechain
Tempo: 120-127 BPM   swing 0.55
Scales: minor, dorian
Chord progressions:
    minor: i - VI - III - VII | i - iv - v - iv | i - VII - VI - VII | VI - iv - i - v
    dorian: i - iv - i - iv | i - iv - VII - v | i - VII - III - iv
Song structure:
    intro      8 bars  chords, kick, pad, top   [filter_in]
    verse     16 bars  arp, bass, chords, kick, snare, top   [crash]
    build      8 bars  arp, chords, pad, top   [build]
    drop      16 bars  arp, bass, chords, kick, lead, pad, snare, top   [drop]
    break      8 bars  chords, lead, pad   [alt, crash]
    build      8 bars  arp, chords, lead, top   [build, alt]
    drop      16 bars  arp, bass, chords, kick, lead, pad, snare, top   [drop]
    outro      8 bars  chords, kick, pad, top   [fade, crash]
    = 88 bars, about 2:51 at 124 BPM   (other layouts it can pick: edm, house_dj, house_song)

    conga    ..o..x.....o.x..   ......x..x....o.

Chord rhythm: ..x...x.x...x..x   ...x..x....x..x.   x..x..x...x..x..
Lead rhythm: x.x.x-..x-x.x-..   x-..x.x-..x.x-..   ..x.x..x..x.x...   x--.x--.x-x-x---
